            records.append(

                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"

    @pytest.fixture
    def case(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
        root = tmp_path / "repository"
        root.mkdir()
        subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q"], cwd=root, check=True)
        subprocess.run(
            [
                "git",
                "-c",
                "user.name=Fixture",
                "-c",
                "user.email=fixture@example.invalid",
                "commit",
                "--allow-empty",
                "-qm",
                "Private publication fixture baseline",
            ],
            cwd=root,
            check=True,
        )
        baseline_commit = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True
        ).strip()
        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),
            (c.SIDECAR, sidecar),
            (c.BASELINE, baseline),

        root.mkdir()

        subprocess.run(
            [
                "git",
                "-c",
                "user.name=Fixture",

                "--allow-empty",
                "-qm",
                "Private publication fixture baseline",
            ],
            cwd=root,
            check=True,
        )
        baseline_commit = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True
        ).strip()
        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),
            (c.SIDECAR, sidecar),
            (c.BASELINE, baseline),

        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),
            (c.SIDECAR, sidecar),
            (c.BASELINE, baseline),

            [
                row(
                    "A",

                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",

                ),

                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )

        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",

        ).strip()
        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )

            (c.POLICY, policy),

tmp_path = PosixPath('/Users/marcin/Documents/VIMSS/ontology/Mechs/MediaIngredientMech/.temp/all-unmatched-20261107/integration/source-index1256-fix-v1/runtime/combined-v1/test_late_drift_never_leaves_s6')
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x146340600>

    @pytest.fixture
    def case(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
        root = tmp_path / "repository"
        root.mkdir()
        subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q"], cwd=root, check=True)
        subprocess.run(
            [
                "git",
                "-c",
                "user.name=Fixture",
                "-c",
                "user.email=fixture@example.invalid",
                "commit",
                "--allow-empty",
                "-qm",
                "Private publication fixture baseline",
            ],
            cwd=root,
            check=True,
        )
        baseline_commit = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True
        ).strip()
        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),
            (c.SIDECAR, sidecar),
            (c.BASELINE, baseline),
>           (c.MAKER, (ROOT / c.MAKER).read_bytes()),
                      ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
            (
                "src/mediaingredientmech/campaign_publication.py",
                (ROOT / "src/mediaingredientmech/campaign_publication.py").read_bytes(),

                "Private publication fixture baseline",
            ],
            cwd=root,
            check=True,
        )
        baseline_commit = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True
        ).strip()
        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),
            (c.SIDECAR, sidecar),
            (c.BASELINE, baseline),

        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",

        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(

            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",

                "-c",
                "user.name=Fixture",
                "-c",

                "commit",
                "--allow-empty",
                "-qm",
                "Private publication fixture baseline",
            ],
            cwd=root,
            check=True,
        )
        baseline_commit = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True
        ).strip()
        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),

        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),
            (c.SIDECAR, sidecar),
            (c.BASELINE, baseline),

        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),

            [

                "-c",
                "user.name=Fixture",
                "-c",
                "user.email=fixture@example.invalid",
                "commit",
                "--allow-empty",
                "-qm",
                "Private publication fixture baseline",
            ],
            cwd=root,
            check=True,
        )
        baseline_commit = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True
        ).strip()
        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",

                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",

                ),

        owner_paths = [

            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",

                "user.name=Fixture",
                "-c",

                "commit",
                "--allow-empty",
                "-qm",
                "Private publication fixture baseline",
            ],
            cwd=root,
            check=True,
        )
        baseline_commit = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True
        ).strip()
        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),
            (c.SIDECAR, sidecar),

tmp_path = PosixPath('/Users/marcin/Documents/VIMSS/ontology/Mechs/MediaIngredientMech/.temp/all-unmatched-20261107/integration/source-index1256-fix-v1/runtime/combined-v1/test_1197_disconnected_prospec7')

    @pytest.fixture
    def case(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
        root = tmp_path / "repository"
        root.mkdir()
        subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q"], cwd=root, check=True)
        subprocess.run(
            [
                "git",
                "-c",
                "user.name=Fixture",
                "-c",
                "user.email=fixture@example.invalid",
                "commit",
                "--allow-empty",
                "-qm",
                "Private publication fixture baseline",
            ],
            cwd=root,
            check=True,
        )
        baseline_commit = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True
        ).strip()
        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),
            (c.SIDECAR, sidecar),
            (c.BASELINE, baseline),
>           (c.MAKER, (ROOT / c.MAKER).read_bytes()),

                "Private publication fixture baseline",
            ],
            cwd=root,
            check=True,
        )
        baseline_commit = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True
        ).strip()
        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),
            (c.SIDECAR, sidecar),
            (c.BASELINE, baseline),

        owner_paths = [

            records.append(

                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"

                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],

                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )

                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path

                row(

                    source="native",

                ),

            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",

        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",

            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],

            cwd=root,
            check=True,
        )
        baseline_commit = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True
        ).strip()
        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),

    @pytest.fixture
    def case(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
        root = tmp_path / "repository"
        root.mkdir()
        subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q"], cwd=root, check=True)
        subprocess.run(
            [
                "git",
                "-c",
                "user.name=Fixture",
                "-c",
                "user.email=fixture@example.invalid",
                "commit",
                "--allow-empty",
                "-qm",
                "Private publication fixture baseline",
            ],
            cwd=root,
            check=True,
        )
        baseline_commit = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True
        ).strip()
        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),
            (c.SIDECAR, sidecar),
            (c.BASELINE, baseline),
>           (c.MAKER, (ROOT / c.MAKER).read_bytes()),

        subprocess.run(
            [
                "git",
                "-c",
                "user.name=Fixture",
                "-c",
                "user.email=fixture@example.invalid",
                "commit",
                "--allow-empty",
                "-qm",
                "Private publication fixture baseline",
            ],
            cwd=root,
            check=True,
        )
        baseline_commit = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True
        ).strip()
        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),
            (c.SIDECAR, sidecar),
            (c.BASELINE, baseline),

        subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q"], cwd=root, check=True)
        subprocess.run(
            [
                "git",
                "-c",
                "user.name=Fixture",
                "-c",
                "user.email=fixture@example.invalid",
                "commit",
                "--allow-empty",
                "-qm",
                "Private publication fixture baseline",
            ],
            cwd=root,
            check=True,
        )
        baseline_commit = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True
        ).strip()
        before = sssom([row("A"), row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:9")])
        candidate = sssom(
            [
                row(
                    "A",
                    other="reviewed alias",
                    source="native",
                    comment="",
                    confidence="0.8",
                    mapping_date="2026-11-08",
                ),
                row("B", "CHEBI:2", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("P", "CHEBI:3", mapping_date="2026-11-08"),
                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )
        sidecar = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\ttoken\towner_subject_ids\tdisposition\tinput_surfaces\tsource_refs\tevidence_refs\n"
        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/B.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/C.yaml",
            "data/ingredients/mapped/P.yaml",
        ]
        for p in owner_paths:
            put(root, p, ("identifier: CHEBI:1\npreferred_term: " + Path(p).stem + "\n").encode())
        after_inventory = inventory(root)
        before_inventory = {
            owner_paths[0]: c.sha(b"old A"),
            owner_paths[2]: c.sha(b"old C"),
            "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml": c.sha(b"old P"),
        }
        records = []
        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):
            old_path = "data/ingredients/unmapped/P_old.yaml" if i == 3 else path
            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",
            artifacts={c.SOURCE: c.sha(before), c.SIDECAR: c.sha(sidecar), c.BASELINE: c.sha(baseline)},
            source_owner_sha256=dict(before_inventory),
            decisions=[{"historic": "unchanged"}],
            policy_evidence=["old evidence"],
            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
        )
        for path, value in [
            (c.SOURCE, before),
            (c.POLICY, policy),
            (c.SIDECAR, sidecar),
            (c.BASELINE, baseline),

                row("Unrelated", "CHEBI:999", source="unrelated native drift"),
            ]
        )

        baseline = b"subject_id\ttoken_key\n"
        owner_paths = [
            "data/ingredients/mapped/A.yaml",

        for i, path in enumerate(owner_paths):

            kind = "create" if i == 1 else "promote" if i == 3 else "edit"
            records.append(
                {
                    "operation_id": str(i),
                    "kind": kind,
                    "source_record": path,
                    "source_path": None if i == 1 else old_path,
                    "destination_path": path,
                    "shape": (
                        "new_record" if i == 1 else "promotion" if i == 3 else "campaign_owner_edit"
                    ),
                    "before_yaml_sha256": before_inventory.get(old_path),
                    "after_yaml_sha256": after_inventory[path],
                    "change": "Fixture reviewed change",
                    "verification": "Fixture evidence; no approval",
                }
            )
            if i == 3:
                records[-1]["before_source_record"] = old_path
        policy = dict.fromkeys(c.POLICY_REQUIRED, "historical")
        policy.update(
            schema_version=1,
            policy_version="owned-name-policy-v1",
            approval="NONE: original historical policy",

            source_alias_addition_updates=[{"old": "history"}],
